### Slide 26

**Text**

Safety
Improving safety performance remains our highest priority
Lag Safety Performance¹
20.2 / 17.7 / 16.5 / 16.0 / 12.7 / 12.0 / 12.1 / 10.4
1Q23 / 2Q23 / 3Q23 / 4Q23 / 1Q24 / 2Q24 / 3Q24 / 4Q24
TRI's / TRIFR
TRIFR has reduced over recent time but remains higher / than desired
We are strengthening our safety culture and leadership / capability through education and coaching
We will continue our focus on occupational health, / wellbeing and psychosocial risk management
1. 12 month moving average calculated as the number of recordable injuries x 1,000,000 divided by the total number of hours worked. IGO reports recordable injuries (TRI) as the sum of Lost Time Injury (LTI), Medically Treated Injury (MTI) / and Restricted Work Injury (RWI) as required by Part 1.1 r.5 of the WHS (Mines) Regulations 2022. Western Areas injury data is captured from June 2022 onwards for the lead indicators, and April 2023 onwards for the lag indicators.
IGO Limited | Strategy Update
Page 25

**Layout**

Single slide with a title block top-left (Safety, with subtitle beneath) and a circular IGO logo top-right. A full-width gradient rule sits at about 23% of the height. Below it, a bar-and-line chart occupies the left column (about 5% to 53% of width) and three statements in navy text with thin orange rules between them occupy the right column (about 52% to 94%). A two-line footnote sits near the bottom, above a footer at bottom-left and a right-aligned page number at bottom-right. Left margin is about 5.3% of width; no slide number other than the footer text Page 25.

**Visuals**

Chart: combined bar and line chart titled Lag Safety Performance with superscript 1. Bars series TRI's in dark navy #2C2D65, unlabelled, no value axis or axis title shown; bars are about 84% (1Q23), 50% (2Q23), 67% (3Q23), 33% (4Q23), 100% (1Q24), 83% (2Q24), 17% (3Q24), 50% (4Q24) of the tallest bar (relative heights are estimates). Line series TRIFR in orange #E36C0A, 2.25pt line with hollow white circular markers outlined in orange, each with printed value: 1Q23 20.2, 2Q23 17.7, 3Q23 16.5, 4Q23 16.0, 1Q24 12.7, 2Q24 12.0, 3Q24 12.1, 4Q24 10.4. Labels are bold black Calibri above markers. Category axis labels 1Q23 to 4Q24 in black Arial beneath a thin grey horizontal baseline; no vertical axis. Legend at bottom centre: navy bar swatch labelled TRI's, then orange line-with-marker labelled TRIFR. No points are highlighted beyond the marker styling. Logo: IGO circular mark with white lowercase igo lettering and a small orange dot above the i. Decorative rule: a horizontal line with a blue-to-grey gradient, about 3 pixels tall.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and chart heading in navy #002060; statements in dark blue #152B6F; subtitle and footer in grey #474B55; footnote in dark grey #42464B. Accents: orange #E36C0A for the TRIFR line and the thin rules between statements; logo circle #152B6F. Typefaces: Aptos Bold for title and chart heading, Aptos regular for subtitle and statements, Calibri (bold for values, regular for superscript) for data labels, Arial for axis labels, footer and footnote. Title about 7.4% of slide height, subtitle and statements about 3.0%, chart heading 2.6%, footnote and footer about 1.5% to 1.8%. Shapes are square with no corner radii or shadows; rules are 1pt-ish orange lines.

**Text**

Sustainability
Delivering our strategy will rely on the trust we hold with all of our stakeholders
Unique culture, outstanding people and a strong focus on / safety, diversity, inclusion and care
Committed to managing our climate and nature-related risks / and opportunities - Net Zero Scope 1 and 2 emissions across / our operated assets by 2035
Focused on driving reconciliation and working with traditional / owners – Innovate Reconciliation Action Plan launched in / August 2023
Safety, Health / and Wellbeing
Our People

Left alignment is at x 5.3% margin throughout.

**Visuals**

One circular ring diagram (rendered as a 1200 by 1200 pixel image, placed at x 54.2%, y 21.6%, w 41.5%, h 73.8%, not cropped, boxed by no frame) made of eight segments in two alternating colours: blue #2B579A segments and dark charcoal #3A3F47 segments, plus beige-grey #C8C2BE segments, arranged clockwise from the top: Safety, Health and Wellbeing (blue, top, with a white heart-with-pulse-line icon), Our People (charcoal, upper right, with a white group-of-three-people icon), Traditional Owners and Communities (blue, right, with a white handshake-in-heart icon), Our Approach to Climate Change (beige, lower right, with a white globe-and-arrows icon), Environment (blue, bottom, with a white hands-holding-sprout icon), Governance and Business Integrity (charcoal, left, with a white speech-bubbles icon), Our Financial Contributions (beige, upper left, with a white dollar-sign icon). Three orange horizontal rules (about #F4A23A, about 0.2% of slide height thick) divide the left column at about y 47.8% and y 68.6%, spanning about x 5.3% to 50%. A thin blue-to-grey gradient rule (image 910 by 3 pixels, x 5.2%, y 23.1%, w 94.8%, h 0.6%) runs across the slide. The IGO logo is a navy #152B6F circle (x 88.5%, y 5.7%, w 6.1%, h 10.9%) containing the white lowercase wordmark 'igo' with an orange dot over the i.

Body paragraphs in regular #152B6F navy Aptos at about 3% of slide height with generous line spacing and wide gaps between paragraphs. Ring labels are small bold white sans-serif. Footer in #474B55 ArialMT at about 1.8% of slide height. Accents are blue #2B579A, orange #F4A23A, and beige-grey #C8C2BE. Shapes are flat with no shadows, gradients only on the top rule.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide with a full-width title block at top left: the title 'Emissions' sits at x 5.3%, y 7.7%, with the subtitle beneath it at y 17.1%, and a thin blue-to-grey gradient rule at y 23.1% spanning the width from x 5.2% to 100%. The left column (x 5.3% to about 42%) stacks three statements separated by two orange horizontal rules at about y 48% and y 69%; the right column (x 47% to 95%) holds the chart title, the waterfall chart image, four green pill labels and a footnote, all left-aligned to the left edge of the footnote. The igo logo sits top right at x 88.5%, y 5.7%; the footer 'IGO Limited | Strategy Update' sits bottom left at y 94.6% and 'Page 27' bottom right at x 88.8%, y 94.5%.

**Visuals**

One waterfall (bar) chart, titled 'Nova Net Zero Roadmap' (bold navy, above the chart). Y-axis title 'Emissions (t CO2-e)', grey, rotated vertically; no numeric tick labels are printed, so the axis range is not stated. X-axis categories left to right: FY19 Baseline, FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, FY24, FY25 Forecast Reductions, FY25 ACCUS. Series: FY19 Baseline is a full green (about #A9D18E) total bar labelled 70,985 at its top. FY20 is an orange (#ED7D31) floating decrease bar, value about -5,000 (not printed). FY21 is a near-zero thin connector (about 0, not printed). FY22 is a small orange decrease bar (about -1,000, not printed). FY23 is an orange decrease bar (about -4,000, not printed). FY24 is a thin blue (about #4472C4) total bar labelled 56,455. FY25 Forecast Reductions is an orange decrease bar (about -4,000, not printed). FY25 ACCUS is a tall orange total bar labelled 52,000 at its top. Thin grey connector lines join the bar tops; no legend; no highlighted points beyond the colour coding. Below the chart are four stacked light-green rounded pills (#D9F2D0) with no outline, right-aligned to x 88.6%: '5.5MW Solar Farm' (widest, x 65.0%, y 72.5%), 'Vent on demand, BESS and EV trials' (x 68.9%, y 76.3%), '10MW solar farm & BESS' (x 76.5%, y 80.2%), and 'Nature Based solutions (ACCUS)' (x 84.6%, y 84.1%, two lines). Each pill is fully rounded at its ends. A small igo logo is a navy circle with white lowercase 'igo' and an orange dot over the i, at top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF). Title 'Emissions' in bold navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height. Subtitle in regular grey #474B55, about 3.0% of slide height. Left-column statements in regular navy #2C2D65 for the first, and #152B6F for the second and third, about 3.0% of slide height. Chart title bold #002060 about 2.6%. Footnote and data labels black #000000 about 1.5% to 2.0%. Pill labels black Arial-style sans about 1.7%. Accent colours: orange rules (#ED9B33 approximately), navy logo circle #152B6F, light-green pills #D9F2D0. Typeface for headings and body is Aptos (humanist sans-serif); pill labels, footer and chart text are Arial-like sans-serif. Weights: bold for title and chart title, regular elsewhere. Lines: orange rules about 2 px thick, flat; the top rule is a blue-to-grey gradient about 0.6% of slide height thick. Pills have full rounded ends and no shadow. No shadows or gradients other than the top rule.

### Slide 29

**Text**

Our Culture / Our culture is a key enabler, connecting our strategy and purpose / Employee insights inform our decisions, shape our / strategies and help to foster a more inclusive culture and / work environment / Our culture is grounded on care, fostering curiosity and / bringing the best out of our people / Delivering our refreshed strategy relies on us continuing to / proactively cultivate a strong sense of connection, / belonging and alignment / Be better / together / We act safely and with care, to the strengths of our people. We empower, support and respect each other. / Ignite / the spark / We seek, question, innovate and create. We know that without a burning curiosity and bright thinking, we risk missing the really big opportunities. / See / beyond / We know that our actions today will impact the world of tomorrow. We believe our people, community and the environment really matter. / Run through / the sprinklers / We find the fun in what we do. When our workplaces are healthier and happier, we are better. / Never / stand still / We are bold, adventurous and excited for the future. We imagine new opportunities and seek new horizons. / IGO Limited | Strategy Update / Page 28

**Layout**

Single-column text on the left and a four-pill icon graphic on the right of a white slide, with the title top-left at about 5% margin and the subtitle directly beneath it. Three navy statements are stacked in the left column, separated by two orange horizontal rules; the footer sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

Two illustration graphics in the right half. Each badge has a two-line bold dark grey #3A3A3A label to the right of the circle and a small grey #474B55 caption beneath it. Lower graphic (bottom-right of body, boxed, no overlay): three capsule badges in a row with the same pill style: a green #5DBB46 circle with a white line globe and figures labelled 'See beyond', a purple #6B4C9A circle with a white line figure leaping through droplets labelled 'Run through the sprinklers', and a crimson #C8215A circle with a white line figure running up steps labelled 'Never stand still'. Each has a grey caption beneath. No photographs; all imagery is flat line-icon illustration. Two thin orange #F5A623 horizontal rules divide the left column.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

**Layout**

Six-person leadership grid in a 3-column by 2-row arrangement beneath a left-aligned title and subtitle, with the logo in the top-right corner. Each person has a circular portrait at left and a text block to its right; a thin blue-to-grey horizontal rule separates the header from the grid. Footer text sits bottom-left with the page number bottom-right, both at the left and right margins (about 5% and 89%).

**Visuals**

Six circular portrait photographs of executives, each in a headshot framing with head and shoulders visible. Ivan Vella: man in white open-collar shirt, smiling, light grey background. Kathleen Bozanic: woman with long dark hair in a blue, teal and purple patterned top, smiling, light grey-blue background. Brett Salt: man in navy blazer and open white collar, neutral serious expression, dark brownish background. Sam Retallack: woman with shoulder-length light brown hair in a black blazer, smiling, light grey background. Marie Bourgoin: woman with long straight hair in a cream jacket and black top with pendant necklace, smiling, white background. Cameron Wilson: man in navy suit with blue patterned tie, smiling, blurred light blue water background. Photos are circular crops with no border. A blue-to-grey gradient horizontal rule runs across the slide below the title area, about 0.6% of slide height thick, spanning from the left margin to the right edge. Logo: igo circular logo, navy #152B6F filled circle with white lowercase 'igo' lettering and an orange dot over the i.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF.

Page 30

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a left text column (about 5% to 42% of slide width) holding three stacked paragraphs separated by two orange rules, and a right diagram column (about 44% to 94%) holding a connected organisation diagram. The title sits top-left at x 5.3%, y 7.7%, with the subtitle beneath it at y 17.1%; a full-width gradient rule runs at y 23.1% from x 5.2% to the right edge. The IGO logo is top-right, and the footer sits bottom-left (x 5.3%, y 94.6%) with the page number bottom-right, right-aligned to about 92.4%. Left margin is about 5.2%; all text is left-aligned.

**Visuals**

Organisation-style diagram with rounded pill and circle shapes linked by thin blue connector lines. Top row: an IGO logo in a light grey pill (#F2F2F2) at about x 55.9%, y 24.6%, and a Tianqi Lithium logo in a light grey pill at about x 74.7%, y 24.6%. A navy (#14284B) pill labelled TLEA in white bold italic sits centred at about x 68.3%, y 41.7%. Left: an Albemarle logo (teal mark with dark blue serif wordmark and registered mark) in a light grey pill (#F2F2F2) at about x 43.9%, y 46.3%. Connector lines (dark blue #1A5C8C, about 2 px) run from the IGO pill down to a blue 49% circle, from the Tianqi pill down to a blue 51% circle, from the Albemarle pill down and right to the 49% circle at the bottom, and from the TLEA box down to a 51% circle and a 100% circle. Blue circles (#0F9ED5, about 4% of slide width, white bold text) are labelled 49% (IGO, top left, centre about 64.6%, 44.7%), 51% (Tianqi, top right, centre about 83.4%, 44.7%), 49% (Albemarle, bottom left, centre about 61.4%, 63.6%), 51% (TLEA lower, centre about 67.5%, 63.6%) and 100% (Kwinana, centre about 83.3%, 63.6%). Bottom left: a white pill with a navy (#14284B) outline containing the Talison Lithium logo (green LTi square mark and navy bold text TALISON LITHIUM), connected by a line from the 49% and 51% circles. Bottom right: a navy (#14284B) pill with a white bold italic TLEA wordmark, connected from the 100% circle. Beneath the two bottom shapes are a green (#4EA72E) label reading Greenbushes and a blue (#0F9ED5) label reading Kwinana Refinery. Top-right IGO logo: navy circle with white lowercase igo and an orange dot over the i. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in dark navy #002060, subtitle in grey #474B55, body text in dark indigo #2C2D65, footer and page number in grey #474B55. Accent colours: orange rules #F0A030, blue gradient header rule from #2F5BB7 fading to light grey, blue #0F9ED5, green #4EA72E, navy #14284B and #152B6F. Typeface is Aptos (a humanist sans-serif) in Bold for title and labels and Regular for body; the footer and page number are Arial in regular. Title is about 7.4% of slide height, body about 2.96%, footer about 1.84%. Shapes have fully rounded pill ends and circular blue badges; connector lines are flat with no shadows. No shadows or gradient fills except the header rule.

### Slide 32

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Plots cost curve position against production so the investor can see where the asset sits on the industry curve.
- Review: The deck has a clear route (market context, purpose and strategy, delivery, summary, appendix), a consistent template and well-built chart slides: slide 6 pairs four plain statements with two readable charts, and slide 32 shows Greenbushes at the bottom of a cost curve.

**Text**

Greenbushes / The world’s largest and lowest cost hard rock lithium mine / TIANQI LITHIUM ENERGY AUSTRALIA / a Tianqi Lithium—IGO joint venture / igo / FY24 Production Results: / • Spodumene Concentrate Production:1.38Mt (SC6) / • Cash Costs (production): $US330/t / • EBITDA Margin: 85% / Clear pathway to 2.0Mt installed processing capacity with CGP3 / expected to be ready for commissioning in September Quarter / 2025 / 23 year mine life based on current ore reserve estimate¹ / 2Q24 – SC6 Cost Curve (C1) / USD per tonne / 1,400 / 1,200 / 1,000 / 800 / 600 / 400 / 200 / - / Greenbushes / - / 500 1,000 1,500 2,000 2,500 3,000 3,500 4,000 4,500 5,000 5,500 / Kt Concentrate / Source: Benchmark Mineral Intelligence, 2024 / 1. Refer to IGO ASX Release titled “Greenbushes CY23 Resources and Reserves”, released 19 February 2024 / IGO Limited | Strategy Update / Page 31

**Layout**

Single slide on a white field with a two-column body. The title Greenbushes sits top-left at x 5.3%, y 7.7% with the subtitle beneath it at y 17.2%; the TIANQI LITHIUM ENERGY AUSTRALIA wordmark and the circular igo logo sit top-right, aligned to the title band. A full-width gradient rule runs at y 23.1% from the left margin to the right edge. The left column (x 5.3% to about 51%) holds the FY24 bullets, two orange rules and two statements, with the footnote at the bottom left. The right column (x 59.1% to 92.4%) holds the cost-curve chart with its caption above and source line below. The footer IGO Limited | Strategy Update sits bottom-left at y 94.6% and Page 31 bottom-right, both at the 5.3% and 88.5% margins.

**Visuals**

One bar chart and two logos. Chart: 2Q24 – SC6 Cost Curve (C1), a step-style cost curve in which each bar's width is its tonnage and its height is cost. Y axis labelled USD per tonne, range 0 to 1,400, gridlines and labels at every 200 (1,400, 1,200, 1,000, 800, 600, 400, 200, and '-' at zero). X axis labelled Kt Concentrate, range 0 to about 5,900, labelled every 500 from '-' (0) to 5,500. Bars are contiguous with thin white separators, all filled #0F9ED5 with no legend. Estimated values, as the slide prints none: first bar Greenbushes at about 290 USD per tonne spanning about 0 to 1,420 Kt, highlighted only by the white bold label 'Greenbushes' inside its base; the next bars rise to about 305, 365, 380, 395, 415 and 435 USD per tonne between about 1,420 and 2,000 Kt; about 500 to 550 USD per tonne between about 2,300 and 3,500 Kt; about 560 to 600 between about 3,600 and 4,600 Kt; about 630 to 860 between about 4,600 and 5,600 Kt; the final bar about 1,140 USD per tonne near 5,900 Kt. Source line under the chart: Source: Benchmark Mineral Intelligence, 2024. Logo 1: TIANQI LITHIUM ENERGY AUSTRALIA over a Tianqi Lithium—IGO joint venture, a raster image 335 by 71 px, dark teal-blue text about #1E6E8C, right-aligned. Logo 2: igo mark, white lowercase lettering with a small orange dot over the i, inside a navy #152B6F circle.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style on a white background. Title Greenbushes in Aptos Bold, navy #002060, large (about 7.4% of slide height). Subtitle in Aptos regular, dark grey #474B55, about 3.0% of slide height. Section heading FY24 Production Results: in Aptos Bold, blue #0070C0, about 3.0%. Body bullets and statements in Aptos regular, dark indigo #2C2D65, about 3.0%; bullet glyphs in Arial, same colour. Chart caption in Aptos Bold black #000000 about 2.2%. Chart labels in Arial, black, about 1.8%. Footnote and source line in Arial about 1.5%, grey #42464B and black. Footer and page number in Arial about 1.8%, grey #474B55. Accent rules: a full-width gradient line about 0.6% of height thick running from blue #0070C0 at left to light grey at right; two orange horizontal rules, about #F0A045, each about 0.3% of height thick, spanning x 5.2% to 51.3% at y 53.2% and y 70.2%. Shapes have square corners and no shadows or gradients other than the rule.

**Layout**

Single-column header at top left with the title Greenbushes and a grey subtitle beneath it; the Tianqi logo wordmark sits right-aligned top right beside the navy igo roundel, followed by a full-width blue-to-grey rule at y 23 percent. Below the rule, a left column (x 5 to 50 percent) holds three text blocks separated by orange rules, and a right column (x 52 to 90 percent) holds the chart title, an EBITDA margin box and the chart. Footer text sits bottom left and the page number bottom right, both at about y 94.5 percent.

**Visuals**

One line chart with two series over twelve quarters, 1Q22 to 4Q24, with category labels 1Q22 2Q22 3Q22 4Q22 1Q23 2Q23 3Q23 4Q23 1Q24 2Q24 3Q24 4Q24 along the horizontal axis. Chart title: Greenbushes cash costs, realised price & EBITDA margin. Series 1, Realised price (US$/t FOB Aus), orange line (about #E8702A) with dark navy circular markers (#152B6F) and printed values: 592, 592, 1,770, 1,770, 3,792, 3,984, 5,783, 5,431, 3,740, 3,016, 1,034, 1,020. Series 2, Cash Cost (production) (A$/t), bright cyan line (#00B0F0) with cyan circular markers and printed values: 185, 205, 214, 215, 224, 226, 253, 271, 262, 357, 386, 338. No visible y-axis and no gridlines; the value scale runs from 0 at the baseline upward, and white-filled tick labels 0 to 6,000 are present but invisible on the white background. Peak point 5,783 in 3Q23 is the highest point. Legend below the axis: orange line with navy dot for Realised price (US$/t FOB Aus), and cyan line with cyan dot for Cash Cost (production) (A$/t). Above the plot, a boxed row titled EBITDA Margin lists 62%, 55%, 78%, 75%, 88%, 88%, 92%, 93%, 91%, 89%, 74%, 68% aligned to the quarters in order. The Tianqi wordmark and igo roundel are logos.

**Design**

Background: Plain solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide with no decoration beyond a thin rule. Title in bold Aptos-style humanist sans-serif in navy #002060, about 7.4 percent of slide height. Subtitle in regular Aptos, grey #474B55, about 3.0 percent. Body statements in regular Aptos, navy #152B6F, about 3.0 percent of slide height; the final question is Aptos Bold in the same navy. Accent rules are orange (about #F0A54A) and the header rule is a blue-to-grey gradient. Chart text black, data labels Arial Bold about 1.8 percent; cash cost labels Aptos Bold in cyan #00B0F0. Square corners, no shadows.

### Slide 34

**Text**

Kwinana / Supporting TLEA to improve production performance from Train 1 / TIANQI LIMITED ENERGY AUSTRALIA / a Tianqi Lithium—IGO joint venture / igo / Performance improved in FY24 with increased reliability, / availability and operating control / TLEA are progressively solving the technical issues we have / encountered through ramp up, with the shut down in / October 2024 expected to deliver the next uplift in / performance / TLC are bringing their extensive experience and expertise to / the rectification programs / Partners will carefully assess economics of Train 2 / expansion once FEED is complete / Train 1 - Lithium Hydroxide Production (Quarterly) / Positive momentum / 4Q22 1Q23 2Q23 3Q23 4Q23 1Q24 2Q24 3Q24 4Q24 / IGO Limited | Strategy Update / Page 33

**Layout**

Single-column text on the left (x 5.3% to about 48%) and a chart block on the right (x about 55% to 92%) on a white field with a 5.3% left margin. The title Kwinana sits top-left at y 7.7%, with the subtitle beneath it at y 17.1%; the TIANQI logo and igo roundel sit top-right (x 60% to 95%), and a full-width gradient rule runs at y 23.1%. Four body paragraphs are separated by orange horizontal rules at y about 43%, 61% and 77%. The footer text sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right at y 94.6%.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled Train 1 - Lithium Hydroxide Production (Quarterly), with vertical blue columns (#0070C0) and no visible value labels; the bars are estimated, about 85, 190, 585, 960, 140, 605, 620, 955 and 1330 respectively, against a y-axis of 0 to 1400 in steps of 200, where the axis labels 0 to 1400 are set in white and are not visible. The x-axis categories are 4Q22, 1Q23, 2Q23, 3Q23, 4Q23, 1Q24, 2Q24, 3Q24 and 4Q24 in black, with a thin black horizontal baseline. A dark teal (#1A5E7A) straight arrow with an arrowhead at the upper right runs diagonally from about the 4Q22 bar up to above the 4Q24 bar, with the black bold rotated label Positive momentum along it. No legend and no highlighted points. Logos: the TIANQI LIMITED ENERGY AUSTRALIA wordmark with the line a Tianqi Lithium—IGO joint venture in teal, and the igo roundel in white on navy.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title Kwinana in bold sans-serif (Aptos Bold) in navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height. Subtitle and footer in grey #474B55 Aptos regular, about 3% for subtitle and 1.8% for footer. Body text in Aptos regular, dark indigo #2C2D65, about 3% of slide height, with generous line spacing. Chart title in bold AptosDisplay navy #002060, about 2.2%. Accent rules: orange (#F5A623) thin horizontal lines and a blue-to-grey gradient rule (#2F5BB7 fading to #C8C8C8). Navy roundel #152B6F with white igo lettering. Bars are flat with no corner radius or shadow.

### Slide 35

**Text**

Nickel Business / Laser focused on safe, stable production and cash maximisation at Nova / Nova / First quartile cost position underpins strong free cash flow until / end of life / Team are focused on opportunities to reduce costs and / accelerate mining by optimising sequence and increasing / instantaneous production rates (equipment and mining cycle) / Delivering operational excellence at Nova will be a key enabler / as we develop our Playbook / Forrestania / Commencing transition to care and maintenance / Cosmos / Study underway to understand opportunities to drive value / Nova Nickel Production (tonnes contained) / ? / ? / 20,000 / 18,000 / 16,000 / 14,000 / 12,000 / 10,000 / 8,000 / 6,000 / 4,000 / 2,000 / 0 / FY25 / FY26 / FY27 / Nickel Production (low) / Nickel Production (high) / Focus on achieving the high end of nickel production in FY25 & / FY26 to reduce the FY27 tail. / IGO Limited | Strategy Update / Page 34

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a left text column spanning about 5% to 51% of the width and a right chart column from about 55% to 93%. The title sits top-left at about 8% from the top with a subtitle directly beneath it, and a thin full-width blue-to-grey rule sits at about 23% height. The logo is top-right, the footer is bottom-left with the page number bottom-right, and the left margin is about 5.3%.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart titled 'Nova Nickel Production (tonnes contained)'. Y axis runs 0 to 20,000 in steps of 2,000, with unit tonnes contained and no axis title. X axis categories are FY25, FY26 and FY27. Series 1 'Nickel Production (low)' is dark teal #156082 at the bottom of each column; about 16,000 for FY25, about 15,500 for FY26 and about 2,500 for FY27 (no values printed). Series 2 'Nickel Production (high)' is orange #E97132 on top; about 18,000 total for FY25, about 17,500 for FY26 and about 5,100 total for FY27 (no values printed). The FY27 orange segment is a diagonal white-striped orange hatch. Legend sits below the axis with two entries: teal square then orange square. Two black arrows with arrowheads at the top: one from the FY27 column (about 87%, 68%) to the top of FY25 (about 65%, 42%), and one from the FY27 column to the top of FY26 (about 76%, 44%). Two red question marks sit between the columns: one above the FY25-FY26 gap and one above the FY27 column. Caption in bold italic beneath the chart. Logo: a navy circle with white lowercase 'igo' lettering, with a small orange dot above the i.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no texture. Title is bold geometric sans-serif (Aptos Bold style) in navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height for cap height. Subtitle is regular sans-serif in dark grey #474B55, about 3% of height. Section headings 'Nova', 'Forrestania' and 'Cosmos' are bold sans-serif in blue #2C5AA8, about 3% of height. Body text is regular sans-serif in dark indigo #2C2D65, about 3% of height. Chart labels are Arial-style sans-serif in black #000000 at about 1.7% of height; legend text is grey #595959. Accents: thin rule in blue #2C5AA8 fading to grey, orange horizontal dividers in #E97132 between Nova statements, and a light grey #F2F2F2 panel behind the Forrestania and Cosmos block. Shapes have square corners with no shadows.

### Slide 36

**Text**

Exploration / We have refocused our exploration capability to drive commercial success and returns / Tenure / People / Relationships / Tools / Large and highly / prospective tenement / package across belt / scale regions in Australia / for our target / commodities / Highly experienced team / with diverse skills across / earth sciences / Strong and collaborative / relationships with / Traditional Owners / Proprietary tools, / technology and data sets / Opportunity / Capability / Access / Leverage / Refocused / Process and Commercial / Agility | Effective allocation of capital | Disciplined Decisions | Returns Focused | Transparency / IGO Limited | Strategy Update / Page 35

**Layout**

Single-slide strategy layout on a white field with the title Exploration top-left at about 5 percent margin, a grey subtitle beneath it, a thin blue-to-grey gradient rule across the width, and the IGO logo top-right. A large rounded navy-outlined panel holds four equal columns (Tenure, People, Relationships, Tools) with a navy left bar containing a check icon; below it a centred green Refocused pill, a down arrow, an orange full-width Process and Commercial bar, and a centred line of five keywords. Footer text sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four columns, each with a navy rounded-rectangle header pill bearing white bold text, a centred navy descriptive paragraph, an orange outlined circle containing a downward arrow, and a bold navy label beneath. Left navy bar holds a white outlined circle with a white check mark. Green pill labelled Refocused flanked by thin green chevron marks pointing outward, above a navy outlined circle with a downward arrow. Full-width orange rounded bar labelled Process and Commercial. Logo: navy filled circle with white stylised lowercase igo lettering and a small orange dot above the i. Rule: thin horizontal line with blue-to-grey gradient, about 0.6 percent of slide height. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title navy #002060, bold sans-serif (Aptos Bold), about 7.4 percent of slide height. Subtitle dark grey #474B55, regular sans-serif, about 3.0 percent. Body and column text navy #152B6F, regular sans-serif, about 2.6 percent. Column labels navy #152B6F bold. Pill headers navy #152B6F with white bold text about 3.0 percent. Accents: orange #F7982A, green #58B947. Footer grey #474B55 in Arial, about 1.8 percent. Shapes have fully rounded pill ends, panel corner radius small, navy outline about 2px.

Page 36

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a left-aligned title block at top left (title and subtitle stacked, left edge about 5.3% from the slide's left margin) and the IGO logo in a navy circle at top right. A thin blue-to-grey rule runs full width below the subtitle at about 23% of slide height. The main area holds a large map of Australia spanning the left and centre, with two circular callouts (WA detail at left, south-west WA detail at centre), and a numbered colour-coded legend in a narrow right column. Footer text sits bottom left and the page number bottom right, both on the same baseline at about 95% of slide height.

**Visuals**

Map of Australia on a white background, with a light grey (#DCDCDC) land silhouette and white ocean. Three WA, NT, SA, QLD, NSW and VIC state labels and a TAS island are in white text on the grey. Two large dark outlined circles (about 1.5 px stroke, #2B2F36) act as magnifier callouts: the left circle enlarges south-west WA and the right circle enlarges the south-west corner of WA near Perth and the Great Australian Bight coast; a small dotted line links the right circle to the main map. Deposit areas are drawn as coloured blobs: red #E8402A for Ni-Cu-Co areas (Fraser Range, Kimberley, Irindina, Nova, Raptor, Western Gawler), teal #1FB5C4 for the Lake Campion REE area, green #3DAA3D for Li2O areas (Ida Valley, Henderson, Bloodwood, South West Terrane, Greenbushes), yellow #F5B700 for Cu-Co areas (Paterson, Copper Coast), dark navy-purple #2E2A5E for Ni-Co areas (Cosmos, Forrestania). Numbered markers are small white circles with dark outline and dark numerals for deposits 1, 4, 6, 7, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 17, 18 and 19, and solid dark grey filled circles with white numerals for 2, 5, 10, 13 and 16 in the callouts. Marker 8 sits on a small outline of Arizona in the USA inset. Marker 9 is a dark grey triangle near the centre of the right callout. Deposit labels in the left callout are dark bold sans-serif: Ida Valley, Henderson, Fraser Range, Lake Campion, Forrestania, South West Terrane, Greenbushes. The map is not cropped, is placed directly on the white background without a box, and has no overlay or tint. The legend beside it uses coloured pill labels: Li2O green #3A9B2F, Li2O-Ni yellow-green #B5C52B, Cu-Mo orange #F7941D, Ni-Co purple #6D4C9F, Cu-Co amber #FDB913, Ni-Cu-Co red #E8442E, REE teal #1BA9B8, each with white bold text.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with navy bold sans-serif title (Aptos Bold, about 7.4% of slide height, navy #002060) and grey regular subtitle (Aptos Regular, about 3% of slide height, #474B55). Body and legend text is dark grey #474B55 in Aptos or similar sans-serif, roughly 1.5% of slide height, names in bold and percentages in regular. Footer and page number are ArialMT-style sans-serif, about 1.84% of slide height, grey #474B55. Accent colour is navy #152B6F for the logo disc and the title. The divider line is a horizontal gradient from blue #3A5BA8 at left to light grey #C8C8C8 at right, about 0.6% of slide height thick. Shapes have no corner radius, shadows or gradients other than the divider; the logo disc is a flat navy circle. Legend pills are rounded rectangles with fully rounded ends.

Page 37

**Layout**

Single-column slide with the title Exploration top-left at about 5% margin, the subtitle directly beneath it, and a circular logo top-right. A full-width gradient rule sits below the header; below it a two-column table of four rows, with bold navy row labels in the left column (about 6% to 30%) and black bullet lists in the right column (from about 31%). Thin orange horizontal rules separate the rows, and a footer with a document name at bottom-left and page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

A table with two columns and four rows, separated by thin orange horizontal rules. Column 1 holds row labels: 'New agile methodology and team structure'; 'New gate-to-gate process'; 'New approach to strategic partnerships'; 'Leaner budget and focus on industry leading $ per discovery metrics'. Column 2 holds bullets: Row 1: Cross functional teams and disciplines to ensure regular gating and prioritisation; Clear accountability for project execution; Fail fast mindset. Row 2: Reshaped portfolio; New ranking system which only progresses targets with line of sight to economic discovery; New external panel of experts to drive commercial discipline. Row 3: Seeking new ways to accelerate discovery via new partnership and joint venture structures. Row 4: FY25 budget 35% lower than FY24 spend, with FY26 budget to be sub $50Mp/a; Focus on targets not drill meters; Market will have clear visibility on metrics of success. Also a logo: a navy #152B6F circle with white lowercase 'igo' lettering and a small orange dot above the i. A thin horizontal gradient line from blue to light grey runs under the header at about 23% height.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White #FFFFFF background. Title in bold sans-serif (Aptos Bold) navy #002060, large. Subtitle in regular Aptos #474B55 grey. Row labels in bold Aptos navy #152B6F; body bullets in regular Aptos black #000000. Accent rules orange thin lines. Header rule is a blue-to-grey gradient bar. Footer in ArialMT #474B55 small. Title about 7.4% of slide height, body about 2.6%, row labels about 2.96%. Square corners, no shadows.

### Slide 39

**Text**

Commercial
We are developing a commercial capability focused on lithium
Relative immaturity of lithium market means price transparency, price
formation and ‘value-in-use’ concepts are yet to be developed
We see an opportunity to work with our partner Tianqi to help develop
these markets to ensure the value of our investments and production
are appropriately recognised
TLEA partnership, underpinned by Greenbushes and Kwinana provides
a unique platform to contribute to the industry development
Lithium Hydroxide Production Flowsheet

Page 38

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a left text column of three statements (x 5.3 to about 55.7 percent) and a right column (x 55.7 to about 94) holding the vertical flowsheet, with the title top-left at y 7.7, subtitle beneath it at y 17.1, and a full-width rule at y 23.1. Footer text sits bottom-left at y 94.6 and the page number bottom-right at x 88.5, both on a shared baseline; the logo is top-right in the corner at x 88.5, y 5.7.

**Visuals**

Vertical process flowsheet titled Lithium Hydroxide Production Flowsheet, bold navy text. Rounded-corner rectangles with thin dark navy outlines, white fill, stacked top to bottom and joined by short orange downward arrows: Beneficiation (detail: Crushing, grinding, DMS, Mag sep, Gravity sep, flotation) with an orange arrow pointing right to Tails; Concentrate (α-spodumene in blue) ; Grinding (297-150 µm); Calcination (~1100°C), followed by the unboxed label β-spodumene in blue; Grinding (150-74 µm); Sulfate roasting (250°C) with an orange arrow pointing left to H2SO4 in red; Leaching with an orange arrow pointing left to CaCO2 in green; S/L separation with an orange arrow pointing right to Residue; then unboxed label Li2SO4aq; Purification; Crystalisation; then unboxed label Li2OH.H2O at the bottom. Ore is an unboxed label at the top. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title Aptos Bold in navy #002060, large (about 7.4 percent of slide height). Subtitle Aptos regular in dark grey #474B55, about 3.0 percent. Body statements Aptos regular in navy #152B6F, about 3.0 percent, sentence case, left aligned. Flowsheet text Aptos Bold in dark navy #0E2841 about 1.8 percent, with sub-labels about 1.1 to 1.3 percent. Accents: blue #0F9ED5 for α and β spodumene, red #FF0000 for H2SO4, green #00B050 for CaCO2, orange #F08A3C for arrows and separators. Header rule is a thin horizontal line in a blue-to-grey gradient from #2F5CB8 to #C8C8C8. Flowsheet boxes have square-ish rounded corners (about 1 percent radius), navy outline about 1 pt, no shadow. Logo is a navy circle with white lowercase igo lettering and a small orange dot above the i.

**Layout**

Single-column diagram on a white slide with the title top-left at the left margin (x 5.3%) and a grey subtitle beneath it; a full-width blue-to-grey gradient rule sits at y 23.1% and the igo logo is top-right. The main flow diagram occupies the left of the body (x 5.3% to 72%) in rows, with a right-hand annotation column starting at x 75.1% aligned to the rows, ending in a footer at y 94.6% with the company name bottom-left and page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Flow diagram with rounded pill shapes and grey connectors. Row 1: a wide blue pill labelled Operating Cash Flow / from Safe and Reliable Operations and a narrower blue pill labelled External Capital / (Debt and Equity). Thin light-grey bracket connectors drop from the Operating Cash Flow pill to the orange pills, with a small grey circle containing a downward chevron at about x 30.2% y 42.7% and a second at about x 64.2% y 42.7% on the External Capital connector. Row 2: six orange pills labelled Sustaining Capital, Exploration, Maintenance of Debt, Investment in Sustainability, Organic Growth and Inorganic Growth, connected by brackets above. A light-grey curved arc connector runs under the orange row to a grey chevron circle at about x 38.8% y 58.9%. Row 3: a wide blue pill labelled Shareholder Returns with two lines of body text. Another grey curved arc connects below it to a grey chevron circle at about x 38.8% y 75.1%. Row 4: three green pills labelled Special Dividends and Share Buybacks, Debt Reduction and Inorganic Growth. Right column: two thin orange horizontal rules at about y 49.7% and y 70.6%, each spanning x 75.1% to 96.9%, separating three annotation text blocks. No photographs or chart axes.

**Design**

Background: #FFFFFF plain white. Clean white background with corporate navy titles, grey subtitle and dark navy-violet annotation text. Blue pills #2C59A8, orange pills #F7921E, green pills #58B946 with white bold labels. Typeface is Aptos (sans-serif), bold for titles and pill headings, regular for body and annotations. Title is about 7.4 percent of slide height, subtitle 2.96 percent, pill labels 2.22 percent, body 1.96 to 2.6 percent, footer 1.84 percent. Pills are fully rounded stadium shapes with no shadows; connectors are thin light grey #D0CCCC lines; blue-to-grey gradient rule under the header.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide with the title top-left at about 5% from the left edge, the subtitle directly beneath it, and the IGO logo circle in the top-right corner. A full-width blue-to-grey rule sits at about 23% of the slide height, below which two charts sit side by side, each with a bold centred chart title above, a legend below and a bold navy caption beneath; the footer (left) and page number (right) sit on the bottom margin at about 94.6% of the height.

**Visuals**

Two combined bar and line charts. Left chart: title 'Dividend History (A$M)'. Columns (series 'Dividends (Fin. year basis)', solid blue #2C5AA8, value labels printed inside the top of each bar in white bold): FY20 65, FY21 76, FY22 76, FY23 560, FY24 280. Line (series 'Dividends (Cumulative) A$M', orange about #F79A2E, 3 pt-style stroke, no markers, value labels above each point in dark grey #404040 bold): FY20 83, FY21 158, FY22 234, FY23 794, FY24 1,074. Categories along the horizontal axis: FY20, FY21, FY22, FY23, FY24 in bold black. The horizontal axis is a thin black line; no visible vertical axis labels. Legend below the axis, two entries with a blue square and an orange line sample. Right chart: title 'Dividends vs Underlying FCF (A$M)'. Columns (series 'Underlying FCF A$M', blue #2C5AA8, white bold value labels inside the bar top): FY20 311, FY21 363, FY22 312, FY23 1,098, FY24 713. Line (series 'Dividend:Underlying FCF', orange about #F79A2E, value labels in dark grey bold above points, percentage): FY20 21%, FY21 21%, FY22 24%, FY23 51%, FY24 39%. The FY23 point of the line is marked with a thin grey leader line from the 51% label to the point. Categories FY20 to FY24 in bold black along the horizontal axis; legend below with blue square and orange line entries. Hidden white-on-white axis tick labels exist in the file (left scale 0, 200, 400, 600, 800, 1,000, 1,200; right scale 0%, 10%, 20%, 30%, 40%, 50%, 60%) but are not visible on the rendered slide, so no visible axis ranges are shown. The slide contains no photographs, icons or other images besides the logo and the rule.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the whole slide. Clean corporate style on white. Title in bold Aptos sans-serif, navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height. Subtitle in regular Aptos sans-serif, dark grey #474B55, about 3.0% of slide height. Chart titles and captions in bold Aptos, black #000000 for chart titles and navy #002060 for captions, about 2.6% of slide height. Chart data labels in bold Aptos about 1.5 to 1.7% of slide height, white inside bars and dark grey #404040 above line points. Category labels bold black, legend text regular black, about 1.7% of slide height. Footer in regular Arial-style sans-serif, grey #474B55, about 1.8% of slide height. Accent colours: blue #2C5AA8 for columns, orange about #F79A2E for lines, navy #152B6F for the logo circle. The horizontal rule is a 3 px tall gradient line fading from blue #2C5AA8 at the left to light grey at the right. Shapes have square corners and no shadows.

**Layout**

Three equal columns (Lithium, Copper, Nickel) sit under a title at top left with a subtitle beneath and a gradient rule across the width, with the logo at top right. Four full-width rounded grey bars stack across the lower part of the slide above a footer line at bottom left and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

No photographs or charts. Three column headers, each a rounded outlined pill containing a filled circle with a symbol: green circle 'Li' with green outline pill 'Lithium'; orange circle 'Cu' with orange outline pill 'Copper'; purple circle 'Li' with purple outline pill 'Nickel'. Thin light-grey divider lines sit between bullet groups in each column. Chevron bullets '›' precede each bullet in the column colour. Four full-width grey rounded bars carry centred statements. Top right is a navy circular logo with white lowercase 'igo' lettering and a small orange dot above the 'i'. A thin horizontal rule runs under the subtitle, blue at left fading to grey at right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold sans-serif (Aptos Bold) navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height. Subtitle and footer in regular sans-serif grey #474B55. Body text in regular sans-serif (Aptos) navy #152B6F at about 2.6% of slide height. Accents: lithium green #58B947, copper orange #F7982A, nickel purple #624B9B. Grey bars #D9D9D9 with fully rounded ends and no shadow. Column pills have 1px outlines in their accent colour, fully rounded ends. Logo circle navy #152B6F. No gradients except the thin header rule.

Page 43

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: a left text column with the title at the top left and the section tracker at the bottom left, and a large photograph occupying the right of the slide. The title sits at about 19.7% from the top with a 5.3% left margin. The logo is top right, with a 6.1% wide circle and its right edge near 94.6%. The footer runs along the bottom at about 94.6% from the top, with the footer text left aligned at 5.3% and the page number right aligned to about 92.4%.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a conveyor belt on a steel lattice truss, seen from below against a clear blue sky. The conveyor runs diagonally from the lower left up to the upper right, ending in a small machinery head with a pole and a few small fixtures and antennas at the top right. Below the belt's far end is a large white-grey stockpile cone rising from the bottom right. The camera looks upward. Lighting is bright, even daylight with no strong shadows. Dominant colours are sky blue about #0A7FD4 fading to about #6FB0E6 near the horizon, silver-grey steel about #B5BCC4, and off-white stockpile about #E8E8E6. The photo is cropped: its top-left and bottom-left corners are curved in a large arc, and it runs off the right and bottom edges of the slide, so it is not boxed. There is no tint or overlay. Logo: the IGO logo, a white lowercase 'igo' wordmark with a small orange dot above the i, inside a dark navy circle #152B6F. Icons: four small section markers in circles, numbered 1 to 4 (described under elements). A thin horizontal image strip spans the width at about 23.1% from the top; it is almost invisible against the white background.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate template. Title is bold geometric sans-serif (Aptos Bold), navy #002060, large at about 11.1% of slide height. Footer and page label are regular sans-serif (Arial-like), grey #474B55, at about 1.8% of slide height. The 'Section' label is Aptos Bold, grey #474B55, about 3.3% of slide height. Section number circles are flat with no outline, no shadow and no gradient: circles 1 to 3 are light warm grey about #DCDAD8 with white numerals, and circle 4 is muted periwinkle blue about #8FA6D2 with a white numeral, marking the current section. Logo circle is navy #152B6F. Photograph has a single large rounded arc on its top-left and bottom-left corners. No shadows or gradients on text or shapes.

### Slide 45

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The summary slide reduces the whole strategy to four short headed statements, which works as a clear closing recap.

**Text**

Summary
Discover | Develop | Deliver – Our path to 2035
Focus on battery materials, particularly lithium
Lithium is our core focus, underpinned by our interest in the world’s best hard-rock lithium mine at Greenbushes. Copper and nickel remain on strategy but with a clear understanding of the market opportunities and challenges.
Clear vision on where value is and how to participate
Primary focus on upstream mining, with carefully considered downstream exposure. A portfolio of exploration, development and operating assets enhanced by refocused commercial capability.
Highly disciplined approach
Focus on returns, efficient capital allocation, disciplined decision making, strong governance and operating excellence.
Well positioned to execute
Unique platform comprising renewed and refocused leadership, healthy balance sheet, strong cash generation and conviction in how we generate value.
IGO Limited | Strategy Update
Page 44

**Layout**

Single slide with a white background; the title Summary sits top-left at the left margin with the subtitle beneath it, and the IGO logo sits top-right. A full-width gradient rule runs under the header at about 23% of the height, and the body is a two-column table (bold left column about 6% to 27% of width, regular right column from about 31% to 90%) with four rows separated by orange hairlines. Footer text sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right, both at about 94.5% of the height.

**Visuals**

Four horizontal orange hairline rules separate the table rows: one at about y 30.9% spanning x 5.4% to 91.3%, one at about y 44.4%, one at about y 62% and one at about y 72.4%, each roughly 1 px thick in orange #F5A623. A horizontal gradient rule (910 by 3 pixels) at x 5.2%, y 23.1%, w 94.8%, h 0.6%, running from blue #2F5BB8 at left to pale grey #C8CBD2 at the right edge, unboxed and extending to the slide edge. Top-right logo: an IGO logo consisting of a navy #152B6F filled circle with white lowercase 'igo' lettering and a small orange dot above the i. No photographs, charts or diagrams.

**Design**

Background plain white #FFFFFF with no gradient on the slide body. Title 'Summary' in bold Aptos sans-serif, navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height. Subtitle in regular Aptos sans-serif, dark grey #474B55, about 3.0% of height. Table left column in bold Aptos, navy #152B6F, about 3.0% of height; right column in regular Aptos, dark indigo #2C2D65, about 3.0% of height. Footer in ArialMT sans-serif grey #474B55 at about 1.8% of height. Accents: orange #F5A623 hairlines and blue-to-grey gradient header rule. Shapes have square corners, no shadows, no rounded corners.

### Slide 46

**Text**

Our Unique Value Proposition
We have a distinct set of credentials to deliver our strategy
Our ability and desire to be diversified
Our deep exploration capability and unique opportunity for growth through discovery
Greenbushes delivering strong free cash flow through the cycle and significant opportunity for optimisation
Our track record of operational excellence
Our strategic partnerships with lithium industry leaders
Our culture which empowers and enables our team to deliver success
Our renewed executive team and refocused corporate team
IGO Limited | Strategy Update
Page 45

**Layout**

Seven pale grey curved segments form a fan or half-ring rising from the bottom edge, with a navy igo semicircle at its centre base. Title is top-left at about 5.3% from the left, with subtitle beneath it and a thin blue-to-grey gradient rule across the slide at about 23% height. Logo sits top-right, footer text bottom-left and page number bottom-right, all on a common left margin of about 5.3%.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a fan of seven curved segments, numbered in reading order from left to right, each carrying one callout. Segment 1 (far left, lowest) reads Our culture which empowers and enables our team to deliver success. Segment 2 reads Our ability and desire to be diversified. Segment 3 reads Our deep exploration capability and unique opportunity for growth through discovery. Segment 4 (centre, tallest) reads Greenbushes delivering strong free cash flow through the cycle and significant opportunity for optimisation. Segment 5 reads Our track record of operational excellence. Segment 6 reads Our strategic partnerships with lithium industry leaders. Segment 7 (far right, lowest) reads Our renewed executive team and refocused corporate team. Each segment is light grey #F2F2F2 with a white gap between segments. Seven orange check-mark icons sit on an inner white ring, one below each segment at about the same angle: approximately at 30% x, 84% y; 34% x, 71% y; 41% x, 62% y; 50% x, 58% y; 58% x, 62% y; 65% x, 72% y; 68% x, 86% y. The central navy semicircle contains the white igo wordmark. No photographs or charts. Flat vector style, no shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with flat vector shapes. Title in bold Aptos sans-serif, navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height for cap-height-based size. Subtitle in regular Aptos, dark grey #474B55, about 3.0%. Callout text in bold Aptos, navy #152B6F, about 2.2%, centred. Accent colours: navy #152B6F for logo and central semicircle, orange for check icons and the small dot in the logo, blue-to-grey gradient rule. Segments #F2F2F2 with no outlines, rounded outer corners of about 1% of slide width, no shadows. Footer in Arial regular, #474B55, about 1.8%.

**Text**

Cautionary Statements & Disclaimer / • This presentation has been prepared by IGO Limited (“IGO”) (ABN 46 092 786 304). It should not be considered as an offer or invitation to subscribe for or purchase any securities in IGO or as an inducement to make an offer or invitation with respect to those securities in any jurisdiction. / • This presentation contains general summary information about IGO. The information, opinions or conclusions expressed in the course of this presentation should be read in conjunction with IGO’s other periodic and continuous disclosure announcements lodged with the ASX, which are available on the IGO website. No representation or warranty, express or implied, is made in relation to the fairness, accuracy or completeness of the information, opinions and conclusions expressed in this presentation. / • This presentation includes forward looking information regarding future events, conditions, circumstances and the future financial performance of IGO. Often, but not always, forward looking statements can be identified by the use of forward-looking words such as "may", "will", "expect", "intend", "plan", "estimate", "anticipate", "continue" and "guidance", or other similar words and may include statements regarding plans, strategies and objectives of management, anticipated production or construction commencement dates and expected costs or production outputs. Such forecasts, projections and information are not a guarantee of future performance and involve unknown risks and uncertainties, many of which are beyond IGO’s control, which may cause actual results and developments to differ materially from those expressed or implied. Further details of these risks are set out below. All references to future production and production guidance made in relation to IGO are subject to the completion of all necessary feasibility studies, permit applications and approvals, construction, financing arrangements and access to the necessary infrastructure. Where such a reference is made, it should be read subject to this paragraph and in conjunction with further information about the Mineral Resources and Ore Reserves, as well as any Competent / Persons' Statements included in periodic and continuous disclosure announcements lodged with the ASX. Forward looking statements in this presentation only apply at the date of issue. Subject to any continuing obligations under applicable law or any relevant stock exchange listing rules, in providing this information IGO does not undertake any obligation to publicly update or revise any of the forward-looking statements or to advise of any change in events, conditions or circumstances on which any such statement is based. / • There are a number of risks specific to IGO and of a general nature which may affect the future operating and financial performance of IGO and the value of an investment in IGO including and not limited to economic conditions, stock market fluctuations, commodity demand and price movements, access to infrastructure, timing of environmental approvals, regulatory risks, operational risks, reliance on key personnel, reserve and resource estimations, native title and title risks, foreign currency fluctuations and mining development, construction and commissioning risk. The production guidance in this presentation is subject to risks specific to IGO and of a general nature which may affect the future operating and financial performance of IGO. / • Quarterly Financial Results are unaudited. All currency amounts are in Australian Dollars unless otherwise noted. Net Cash is cash balance less outstanding debt, Net Debt is outstanding debt less cash balances. / • Nickel cash costs are reported inclusive of royalties and after by-product credits on a per unit of payable metal basis, unless otherwise stated. / • Lithium cash costs reported as COGS (cash costs of goods sold) per tonne sold are inclusive of ore mining costs, processing, general and administrative, selling & marketing and inventory movements. / • Lithium cash costs (production) are reporting inclusive of mining, processing, crushing and site administration, and utilise production as a unit of measurement. This measure excludes inventory adjustments, non-site general and administrative, offsite and royalty costs. / Underlying EBITDA is a non-IFRS measure and comprises net profit or loss after tax, adjusted to exclude income tax expense, finance costs, interest income, asset impairments, gain/loss on sale of investments, depreciation and amortisation and other once-off transaction and integration costs. Underlying EBITDA includes IGO’s share of TLEA net profit after tax. / • Free Cash Flow comprises Net Cash Flow from Operating Activities and Net Cash Flow from Investing Activities. Underlying adjustments exclude acquisition and integration costs, proceeds from investment sales, payments for investments and mineral interests and other once-off receipts/payments. / • IGO has a 49% interest in Tianqi Lithium Energy Australia Pty Ltd (TLEA) and therefore, as a non-controlling shareholder, recognises its share of Net Profit After Tax of TLEA in its consolidated financial statements. As such, IGO has provided additional information on the operating, financial and expansion activities at both Greenbushes and the Kwinana Refinery which reflects IGO’s understanding of those operating, financial and expansion activities based on information provided to IGO by TLEA. / IGO Limited | Strategy Update / Page 46

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at the left margin (about 5.3% from the left edge) above a full-width thin gradient rule at about 23% of the height. Body copy is set in three text columns of roughly equal width starting at about 6%, 35% and 65% of the slide width, with a logo circle at top right and a footer line at bottom left and page number at bottom right, both at about 94.6% of the height.

**Visuals**

Non-text elements: one circular navy logo badge at top right containing the white lowercase IGO wordmark with a small orange dot above the i; one thin horizontal gradient rule running from about 5% to 100% of the slide width beneath the title, shifting from medium blue on the left to light grey on the right. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with navy title, dark grey body text in Arial-style sans-serif, and a navy logo badge with a blue-to-grey gradient rule. Title is bold sans-serif (Aptos Bold) at about 7.4% of slide height; body text is regular Arial at about 1.67% of slide height with tight line spacing and bullets in the same grey. Footer and page number are Arial at about 1.84% of slide height. No shadows or corner radii apart from the circular logo badge.

### Slide 48

**Text**

igo / Appendix / IGO Limited | Strategy Update / Page 47

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white field with a 5.3% left margin. The title 'Appendix' sits top-left at about 20% to 31% of the slide height, and the photograph fills the right side from 35.6% to the right edge, starting at 22.2% from the top and ending near 90.7%. The circular logo is top-right at 88.5% to 94.6% horizontally, and the footer runs along the bottom, with the left footer text at the 5.3% margin and the page number starting at 88.5%.

**Visuals**

One photograph and one logo. Photograph: two workers in hi-vis orange and navy work clothing with white hard hats walk away from the camera along a dirt haul road, beside a long steel overland conveyor with yellow-painted handrails and mesh guarding that rises diagonally from lower left to upper right. Lamp posts, gantry steelwork and processing structures are visible on the right, and a clear blue sky fills the upper area. The lighting is bright direct sunlight. Dominant colours are blue sky, orange hi-vis, yellow-brown conveyor, and dark brown ground. The photo is cropped to a shape with a large rounded top-left and bottom-left corner, and its right and bottom edges bleed off the slide. It is not boxed and has no overlay or tint. Logo: a navy circle containing a white lowercase 'igo' wordmark with an orange dot above the 'i'. A thin 910 by 3 pixel image strip sits at 5.2% x, 23.1% y, spanning 94.8% of the width; it is largely hidden behind the photograph and not clearly visible on the render.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF). The title is bold sans-serif (Aptos Bold) in navy #002060 at about 11.1% of slide height for the cap-height box, with a visible descender in 'pp'. The footer and page number are regular Arial-style sans-serif in grey #474B55 at about 1.8% of slide height. The logo is a solid navy #152B6F circle. Page 48

**Layout**

Three columns by three rows of director profiles on a white page. Title sits top-left at about 7.7% from the top, with a grey subtitle beneath it and a logo at top-right; a thin full-width gradient rule sits at about 23% of the height. Each profile is a circular headshot at left with its text block to the right; the three columns start at about 5.3%, 35.7% and 65.9% of the width, rows at about 25.4%, 47.6% and 71.0%. Left-aligned text, left margin about 5.3%, footer line bottom-left and page number bottom-right at about 94.5% of the height.

**Visuals**

Nine circular headshot photographs, each about 10.2% of slide width, arranged in a 3x3 grid, each cropped to a circle with no border or overlay. Row 1: Michael Nossal, a bald smiling man in a light blue open-necked shirt against a pale grey-blue backdrop, head-and-shoulders framing; Ivan Vella, a smiling man with short dark hair in a white open-collar shirt against pale grey; Keith Spence, a smiling man with grey hair and rimmed glasses in a light blue shirt against pale grey. Row 2: Trace Arlaud, a smiling woman with short blonde hair in a black top against pale grey; Debra Bakker, a smiling woman with shoulder-length reddish-brown hair, pearl necklace and light blue top against pale grey; Samantha Hogg, a smiling woman with shoulder-length grey-brown hair, white jacket and gold pendant against pale grey. Row 3: Justin Osborne, a smiling man with short grey-brown hair and beard in navy jacket and white open shirt against pale grey; Xiaoping Yang, a smiling woman with dark hair and glasses in a pink top and black jacket against pale grey; Marcelo Bastos, a smiling man with short grey hair and glasses in a dark suit with light blue tie against pale grey. Lighting is soft and even, framing is head-and-shoulders, all photographs share the same pale grey-blue backdrop. Logo: the igo logo, a white lowercase 'igo' wordmark with an orange dot above the 'i' on a navy circle, at top-right. Rule: a thin horizontal gradient line running from blue to light grey across the full content width.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF). Title in bold sans-serif navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height (cap height about 5.2%). Subtitle in regular sans-serif grey #474B55 at about 3.0%. Director names in bold sans-serif caps, dark indigo #2C2D65, about 2.2%. Roles in bold sans-serif orange #F7982A, about 2.0%. Committee and body lines in regular black #000000 sans-serif, about 2.0%. Tenure lines in italic black #000000, about 2.0%. Footer and page number in grey #474B55, about 1.8%. Logo circle navy #152B6F. Rule is a blue gradient (blue #3A63B8 to light grey) about 0.6% tall. No corner radii, shadows or gradients on the photos; the photos are circular crops.

### Slide 50

**Text**

Commercial
Develop our commercial capability in lithium through our partnership with TLEA
Commercial
IGO seeks to maximise value for produced tonnes and contribute to the development of the lithium
industry. To achieve this we:
•
Must develop appropriate views and skills to shape the industry
Commerciality will provide us with the technical skills and market structure to maximise value
•
It will further develop our technical marketing capability
•
It will be done in collaboration with others to develop clear views on pricing mechanisms
•
It will involve programs to get closer to customers, users and competitors
In practice commerciality will
•
Have Greenbushes generating reliable cashflows through the cycle
•
Enable us to contribute to the shaping of the lithium industry by collaborating with others
Industry
development
Technical
skills
•
Ensure that the value of our investments and production are appropriately recognised
•
Have established us as a globally relevant lithium player
Developed
reputation
for market
leadership
IGO Limited | Strategy Update
Page 49

**Layout**

Single-column text block on the left with a three-ring circular cycle diagram on the right, inside a large light-grey rounded panel. Title sits top-left at about 7.7% from top with a subtitle below it, a full-width blue-to-grey rule beneath, and the IGO logo top-right; footer text bottom-left and page number bottom-right, both left-aligned to the panel margins.

**Visuals**

One diagram: a cycle of three interlocking C-shaped arrows arranged vertically. Top arc is dark navy #152B6F with its arrowhead pointing down-right, labelled 'Industry development'. Middle arc is orange #F7982A with its arrowhead pointing down-right into the bottom arc, labelled 'Technical skills'. Bottom arc is navy #152B6F with its arrowhead at upper-left area pointing back toward the middle, labelled 'Developed reputation for market leadership'. Arcs are thick (about 2.5% of slide width), with thin white outlines where they meet. Each arrow flows from the previous circle into the next, forming a continuous loop. No photographs or logos other than the IGO logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold sans-serif (Aptos Bold) navy #002060, large (about 7.4% of slide height for the title). Subtitle regular Aptos in dark grey #474B55, about 3.0% of slide height. Section tag pill in orange #E97132 with white bold Aptos text. Body headings in bold Aptos navy #152B6F, about 2.6% of slide height; body bullets in regular Aptos black #000000, same size. Panel is light grey #F2F2F2 with rounded corners (small radius, about 1.5% of width) and no shadow. Horizontal rule is a thin gradient line from blue #2F5BB7 to light grey #C8C8C8, about 0.3% of slide height. Logo is a navy #152B6F circle with white 'igo' lettering and an orange dot accent. Footer and page number in regular Aptos grey #474B55, about 1.84% of slide height.

### Slide 51

**Text**

Technical
Develop our IP and capability in building projects fast, with low capital intensity and optimised flow sheets
Technical
IGO seeks to optimise the speed and cost of project development, through technical capability. To
achieve this we:
•
Must develop our IP and capability in building with high speed of delivery, low capital intensity and
optimised flow sheets
The technical approach to project development will create a low-cost pipeline for us
•
It will put innovative lithium processing technology at the forefront, to feed into our studies and
growth through both organic and inorganic activity
•
It will ensure that we are ready to invest for opportunities that arise
In practice our approach to project development will be underpinned by technical expertise
•
To build out a low-cost production pipeline
•
Create a strong lithium business for us with multiple assets across mining stages
Optimised
development
Innovative
technology
Low-cost
pipeline
IGO Limited | Strategy Update
Page 50

**Layout**

Single content slide on a white field. The title sits top-left at the top margin, with the subtitle beneath it and a thin blue-to-grey rule across the full width below that; the navy igo logo disc sits top-right. A content panel fills the lower body from about 5% to 95% of the width, with a left text column of body copy and a right column holding a three-part circular diagram. The footer line sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right, both left-aligned to the margins.

**Visuals**

Diagram: three thick ring segments arranged in a vertical clockwise flow, all on the right side of the grey panel. Top segment is navy #152B6F with its label 'Optimised development' inside its ring. Middle segment is orange #F7982A on the left side of the flow, labelled 'Innovative technology' in the centre. Bottom segment is navy #152B6F with the label 'Low-cost pipeline' inside. Each segment ends in a small arrowhead that feeds into the next segment; the segments overlap slightly at their junctions and have thin white outlines. Logo: igo logo, white lowercase lettering with an orange dot over the i, inside a navy circle at top-right. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background is plain white #FFFFFF. Title is bold Aptos (sans-serif), navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height. Subtitle is regular Aptos sans-serif in dark grey #474B55, about 3.0% of slide height. Orange pill label is bold Aptos white text on #E97132. Body headings are bold Aptos in navy #152B6F, about 2.6% of slide height; body bullets are regular Aptos in black #000000 at the same size. Diagram labels are bold Aptos black, about 2.2%. Accents are orange #E97132, orange #F7982A and navy #152B6F. The content panel is #F2F2F2 with rounded corners of about 1.5% of slide height and no shadow. The rule under the subtitle is a thin gradient bar from blue to light grey.

### Slide 52

**Text**

Partnerships
Continue to develop our lithium focused joint venture TLEA and seek other strategic partners to share risk  & return
Partnerships
IGO will continue to seek strategic partners to share risk and return. To achieve this we:
•  Further develop our lithium focused TLEA joint venture, and
•  Seek out strategic partners where there are mutually beneficial value accretive opportunities
Our approach to partnerships will improve the way we work
•  It will enable us to be more cost effective and efficient in the way we conduct operations
•  It will provide us with new skills and knowledge that we will utilise to reach full potential
In practice we will
•  Have a portfolio of different partners in all stages of the mining value chain
•  Have a successful partnership structure and model that is utilised to determine who we partner with and how we partner

**Layout**

Single-column content slide on a white field with a title top-left at about 7.7% from the top and a subtitle beneath it. The igo logo sits top-right in a circle, a thin blue-to-grey gradient rule runs under the subtitle at about 23% height, and an orange pill label sits below the rule. A large light-grey rounded panel holds the bulleted text at left and a three-arc cycle diagram at right; footer text sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right, both at about 94% height, with margins of about 5% left and right.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a three-stage circular cycle made of three thick C-shaped arcs with arrowheads, stacked vertically on the right of the grey panel. The top arc is navy #152B6F, spanning about x 78.2 to 90.4 and y 38.6 to 61.7, with its arrowhead pointing down-right toward the middle; its label reads 'Value accretive opportunities' in black bold, centred in the arc's inner circle. The middle arc is orange #F7982A, spanning about x 73.7 to 84.7 and y 52.1 to 76.3, with its arrowhead pointing down-right toward the bottom arc; its label reads 'Robust partnering model' in black bold, centred in the middle. The bottom arc is navy #152B6F, spanning about x 78.2 to 91.0 and y 66.5 to 90.3, with its arrowhead pointing up-left into the middle arc, and its label reads 'Maximised operational potential pipeline' in black bold, centred in its inner circle. Each arc has a thin white outline separating it from neighbouring arcs. Logo: a navy #152B6F filled circle at top right containing a white lowercase 'igo' wordmark with a small orange dot above the i. Pill: an orange #E97132 rounded rectangle containing the white bold word 'Partnerships'. Rule: a horizontal line image, about 910 by 3 pixels, running from the left margin to the right edge, shading from blue at left to pale grey at right.

**Design**

### Slide 53

**Text**

Playbook / Differentiating IGO - the interconnected way we work & create value to achieve the expectations of our stakeholders / Playbook / IGO seeks to differentiate itself as the developer and operator of choice for battery materials. To / achieve this we: / • Must have exceptional business discipline, and / • It must be underpinned by a strong culture / The Playbook will describe the interconnected way we do work & create value / • It will describe the principles for how we will approach our work / • It will describe the practices for how we are expected to work / • It will provide all employees and contractors with a shared and consistent way for how everyone / experiences work at IGO / • We believe it will enable our people to understand our whole business and empower them to behave / like owners / In practice the Playbook will / • Ensure a standardised and consistent approach to work within the business / • Ensure we have one best way of working, which becomes the baseline for any improvements / • Be replicable and implemented at new IGO businesses / Exceptional / Business / Discipline / Underpinned / by a Strong / Culture / Differentiates / IGO as a / Developer & / Operator / IGO Limited | Strategy Update / Page 52

**Layout**

Single content slide on a white background with a left-aligned title block at the top left, a logo circle at top right, and a thin divider line below the subtitle. A rounded light-grey panel fills the lower body area with bulleted text in the left column and a three-arc circular cycle diagram in the right column. Left margin is about 5% of slide width; the footer line sits at the bottom left and the page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Diagram: a circular cycle of three thick arcs stacked vertically on the right side of the grey panel, each arc an open ring with an arrowhead, forming a flowing sequence from top to bottom. Top arc is navy #152B6F with the label 'Exceptional Business Discipline' inside its ring, pointing down-right into the middle. Middle arc is orange #F7982A with the label 'Underpinned by a Strong Culture' inside, with its arrowhead pointing down-right into the bottom arc. Bottom arc is navy #152B6F with the label 'Differentiates IGO as a Developer & Operator' inside, closing the sequence. Labels are black bold text centred in the ring interiors. Arcs have thin white outlines separating them. No photographs, charts or logos other than the logo circle at top right, which is a navy circle with a white stylised lowercase 'igo' wordmark and a small orange dot above the 'i'.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title 'Playbook' in bold sans-serif (Aptos Bold style) in navy #002060, about 7.4% of slide height. Subtitle in regular sans-serif (Aptos) in dark grey #474B55, about 2.8% of slide height. Orange pill tag with bold white text 'Playbook' in Aptos Bold about 3.0% of height. Section headings in bold navy #152B6F, about 2.6% of height. Body bullets in regular black #000000 Aptos, about 2.6% of height. Diagram labels in bold black Aptos about 2.2% of height. Footer and page number in Aptos regular #474B55 about 1.8% of height. Divider line is a thin gradient line from medium blue to light grey, about 0.6% of height. Grey panel #F2F2F2 with rounded corners of moderate radius and no shadow. Pill has fully rounded ends, fill #E97132, no outline. Logo circle navy #152B6F.

### Slide 54

**Text**

Contact / Investors & Media / Richard Glass / Head of Corporate Affairs & Investor Relations / T:+6 8 9238 8300 / E: Richard.glass@igo.com.au / IGO Limited | Strategy Update

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: left text column starts at about 5.3% from the left edge with the title 'Contact' near the top-left and contact block in the middle-left; a large rounded-corner photograph fills the right side bleeding off the right edge and sits below the logo. The IGO logo sits top-right and the footer line sits bottom-left at about 94.6% from the top.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a mining processing plant at dusk: a long curved conveyor with orange safety railings running diagonally from lower-left to upper-centre, steel lattice towers and walkways in the centre and right, small floodlights glowing amber along the structures, a pink-to-orange sunset sky behind, and dark brown ground in the foreground with a yellow-and-red bollard and rope barrier at lower-left. Composition is a landscape crop, cropped at the right edge (full-bleed to the right), with the left edge shaped as a large rounded curve (about 20.5% to 100% horizontally, 20.5% to 100% vertically). Lighting is warm low-angle sunset light with artificial lamps; dominant colours are dark charcoal #2B211D, amber #E8962E and sunset pink-orange #D9735A. No overlay or tint beyond natural colour. Logo: IGO corporate logo, white lowercase letters 'igo' with a small orange dot above the 'i' inside a navy filled circle #152B6F, at x 88.5%, y 5.7%, w 6.1%, h 10.9%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with navy title and black body text in Aptos sans-serif. Title 'Contact' is Aptos Bold in navy #002060 at about 11.1% of slide height (large cap height). Body text is Aptos at about 3.3% of slide height, with 'Investors & Media' in Aptos Bold. Email address is teal-grey #467886 with underline. Footer is ArialMT in grey #474B55 at about 1.8% of slide height. No shapes other than the logo circle; the photograph has a large rounded left edge; no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 55

**Review notes**

- Review: Several pages run too small for reading in a room, notably slide 9's wheel diagram, slide 47's disclaimer, slide 55's small text on a pale background, and parts of slides 22 and 37.

**Text**

Making a
Difference
We believe in a world where people power makes
amazing things happen.
Where technology opens up new horizons and clean
energy makes the planet a better place for
generations to come. Our people are bold,
passionate, fearless and fun –we are a smarter,
kinder and more innovative company.
Our work is making fundamental changes to the way
communities all over the world grow, prosper and
stay sustainable. Our teams are finding and
producing the products that will make energy storage
mobile, efficient and effective enough to make long-
term improvements to the lifestyle of hundreds of
millions of people across the globe.
How? Developments in battery storage technology
are enabling the full potential of renewable energy to
be realised, by allowing energy produced from the
sun, wind and other sources to be stored and used
when and where it’s needed. This technology will
impact future generations in ways we cannot yet
imagine, improving people’s quality of life and
changing the way we live.
We believe in a green energy future and by delivering
the products needed for tomorrow’s battery systems,
we are making it happen.
We are the IGO Difference.

**Layout**

Two-column composition on a full-bleed gradient. The large title 'Making a / Difference' sits left of centre on two lines, vertically centred around the middle of the slide, left edge at 6.7% of width. Body copy occupies two narrow left-aligned columns starting at 43.4% and 67.2% of width, top of text at 39.5% of height. The igo logo sits in the top-right corner at about 91.6% to 98.1% of width. No footer, no page number, no subtitle.

**Visuals**

Non-text elements: (1) a full-bleed soft gradient background image covering the whole slide, pale sky blue at top, lavender and periwinkle toward the bottom and right, with a faint lighter blue glow near the top centre; (2) the igo logo in the top-right corner: a navy #152B6F filled circle about 6.5% wide and 11.5% tall, with the white lowercase letters 'igo' on it and a small orange dot above the 'i'; a second navy #152B6F filled shape with bounding box x 88.5, y 5.7, w 6.1, h 10.9 underlies the wordmark; (3) a hairline image 910 by 3 pixels at x 5.2, y 23.1, w 94.8, h 0.6, which is not visibly rendered against the background. No charts, photos or icons.

**Design**

Background is a soft diagonal gradient: pale sky blue near #C5DDF0 at top, light blue #A9D6EE near the top centre, pale grey-blue #C2CCE3 at left, lavender #A59BC9 toward bottom-right and periwinkle #6A86C4 at the bottom-right corner. Title text colour is dark indigo #2C2D65, regular weight, Aptos sans-serif, about 11.1% of slide height per line with tight leading. Body text is dark slate grey #42464B, Aptos regular sans-serif, about 1.7% of slide height, line spacing about 2% of slide height with paragraph gaps. The closing line 'We are the IGO Difference.' is Aptos Bold in indigo #2C2D65 at about 2.0% of slide height. Logo navy #152B6F with white letters and orange accent dot. No shapes, rules, corner radii, shadows or borders.
